32   protected[quality] def literalsNeeded(pos: Int, typ: String = "String"): Nothing =
33     if (pos == -1)
34       qualityException("Cannot setup Quality Expression with non-literals")
35     else
36       qualityException(s"Quality Expression requires a $typ literal in (starts with position 0) position $pos")
37 
38   protected[quality] def literalsNeeded: Nothing = literalsNeeded( -1 )
39 
40   protected[quality] def getLong(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
41     exp match {
42       case Literal(seed: Long, LongType) => seed
43       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Long")
44     }
45   protected[quality] def getInteger(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
46     exp match {
47       case Literal(seed: Int, IntegerType) => seed
48       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Integer")
49     }
50   protected[quality] def getBoolean(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
51     exp match {
52       case Literal(seed: Boolean, BooleanType) => seed
53       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Boolean")
54     }
55   protected[quality] def getString(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) =
56     exp match {
57       case Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) => str.toString()
58       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos)
59     }
60 
61   protected[quality] def getBinary(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1): Array[Byte] =
62     exp match {
63       case Literal(ar: Array[Byte], _: BinaryType) => ar
64       case _ => literalsNeeded(pos, "Binary")
65     }

67   protected[quality] val mustKeepNames = Set(LambdaFunctions.PlaceHolder,
68     LambdaFunctions.Lambda, LambdaFunctions.CallFun)

89   private val noopAdd = (dt: DataType) => None
90 
91   /**
92    * Provides the default monoidal add for a dataType, used for merging summed results when aggregating
93    *
94    * @param dataType
95    * @return
96    */
97   def defaultAdd(dataType: DataType, extension: DataType => Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] = noopAdd): Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] =
98     dataType match {
99       case _: MapType => Some((left, right) => MapMerge(Seq(left, right), (dataType: DataType) => defaultAdd(dataType, extension)))
100       case _: IntegerType | LongType | DoubleType =>
101         Some((left, right) => add(left, right, null))
102       case a: DecimalType =>
103         Some((left, right) => add(left, right, a))
104       case _ => extension(dataType)
105     }
106 
107   /**
108    * Provides the default monoidal Zero for a dataType, used for defaults when aggregating
109    *
110    * @param dataType
111    * @return
112    */
113   def defaultZero(dataType: DataType): Option[Any] =
114     dataType match {
115       case _: MapType => Some(EmptyMap)
116       case _: IntegerType | LongType => Some(0L)
117       case _: DoubleType => Some(0.0)
118       case d: DecimalType => Some(Decimal.createUnsafe(0, d.precision, d.scale))
119       case _ => None
120     }
121 
122   /**
123    * Wrap to provide the default lookup for registerFunctions to change type parsing from DDL based to other or
124    * when None to add additional lookups should ddl fail
125    *
126    * @param string
127    * @return
128    */
129   def defaultParseTypes(string: String): Option[DataType] =
130     try {
131       Some(
132         DataType.fromDDL(string)
133       )
134     } catch {
135       case _: Throwable => None
136     }
137 
138   // #12 - use underscore names but keep the old camel case approach around for compat
139   def registerWithChecks(registerFunction: (String, Seq[Expression] => Expression) => Unit, name: String, argsf: Seq[Expression] => Expression, paramNumbers: Set[Int] = Set.empty, minimum: Int = -1) = {
140     val create =
141       if (paramNumbers.isEmpty && minimum == -1 )
142         argsf
143       else
144         (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
145           if ((paramNumbers.nonEmpty && !paramNumbers.contains(exps.size)) || (minimum > exps.size)) {
146             val sizeerr =
147               if (paramNumbers.nonEmpty)
148                 s"Valid parameter counts are ${paramNumbers.mkString(", ")}"
149               else
150                 s"A minimum of $minimum parameters is required."
151             throw QualityException(s"Wrong number of arguments provided to Quality function $name, received ${exps.size}. $sizeerr")
152           }
153           argsf(exps)
154         }
155 
156     registerFunction(name, create)
157 
158     if (!mustKeepNames(name)) {
159       registerFunction(name.replaceAll("_",""), create)
160     }
161   }
162 
163   /**
164    * Must be called before using any functions like Passed, Failed or Probability(X)
165    * @param parseTypes override type parsing (e.g. DDL, defaults to defaultParseTypes / DataType.fromDDL)
166    * @param zero override zero creation for aggExpr (defaults to defaultZero)
167    * @param add override the "add" function for aggExpr types (defaults to defaultAdd(dataType))
168    * @param writer override the printCode and printExpr print writing function (defaults to println)
169    * @param registerFunction function to register the sql extensions
170    */
171   def registerQualityFunctions(parseTypes: String => Option[DataType] = defaultParseTypes,
172                                zero: DataType => Option[Any] = defaultZero,
173                                add: DataType => Option[(Expression, Expression) => Expression] = (dataType: DataType) => defaultAdd(dataType),
174                                mapCompare: DataType => Option[(Any, Any) => Int] = (dataType: DataType) => utils.defaultMapCompare(dataType),
175                                writer: String => Unit = println,
176                                registerFunction: (String, Seq[Expression] => Expression) => Unit =
177                                 (n, f) => ShimUtils.registerFunction(SparkSession.active)(n,f)
178                               ) : Unit = {
179 
180     def register(name: String, argsf: Seq[Expression] => Expression, paramNumbers: Set[Int] = Set.empty, minimum: Int = -1) =
181       registerWithChecks(registerFunction, name, argsf, paramNumbers, minimum)
182 
183     def parse(exp: Expression) = {
184       val Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) = exp // only accept type as string
185       parseTypes(str.toString).getOrElse(qualityException(s"Could not parse the type $str"))
186     }
187 
188     def getMap(exp: Expression, pos: Int = -1) = exp match {
189       case l: Literal if l.dataType.isInstanceOf[MapType] =>
190         MapUtils.toScalaMap(l.value.asInstanceOf[ArrayBasedMapData], StringType, StringType).map(p => (p._1.toString, p._2.toString))
191       case c: CreateMap if c.children.grouped(2).forall{
192         case Seq(Literal(_: UTF8String, StringType), _: Literal) =>
193           true
194         case _ => false
195       } =>
196         c.children.grouped(2).map{
197           case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), value: Literal) =>
198             str.toString -> value.value.toString()
199         }.toMap
200       case _ => throw QualityException(s"Could not process a literal map with expression $exp index $pos")
201     }

250     register(LambdaFunctions.PlaceHolder, {
251       case Seq(e, Literal(bol: Boolean, BooleanType)) =>
252         PlaceHolderExpression(parse(e), bol)
253       case Seq(e) =>
254         PlaceHolderExpression(parse(e))
255       case _ =>
256         PlaceHolderExpression(LongType)
257     }, Set(0, 1, 2))

259     /* Note - both Lambda and CallFun are only called in top level expressions,
260           nested calls are handled within the lambda expression
261           creation that "calls" this. */
262 
263     register(LambdaFunctions.Lambda, {
264       case Seq(fun: FunForward) =>
265         val res = FunCall(fun)
266         res
267       case Seq(fun: FunN) =>
268         // placeholders that are 1:1
269         val res = fun.function
270         res
271     }, Set(1))
272 
273     register(LambdaFunctions.CallFun, {
274       case (fun@ FunN(_, l@ SLambdaFunction(ff : FunForward, _, _), _, _, _, _)) +: args =>
275         processTopCallFun(fun, l, ff, args)
276       case t => qualityException(s"${LambdaFunctions.CallFun} should only be used to process partially applied functions returned by a user lambda, got $t instead")
277     }, minimum = 1)

279     val afx = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
280       val (sumType, filter, sum, count) =
281         exps.size match {
282           case 3 =>
283             // attempt to take a look at exps1 to identify if it's a FunN or mapWith
284             val typ =
285               exps(1) match {
286                 case FunN(Seq(RefExpression(dataType, _, _)), _, _, _, _, _) => dataType // would default to long anyway
287                 case MapTransform(RefExpression(t: MapType, _, _), _, _, _) => t
288                 case _ => LongType
289               }
290             (typ, exps(0), exps(1), exps(2))
291           case 4 =>
292             val Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType) = exps(0) // only accept type as string
293             if (str.toString == "NO_REWRITE")
294             // signal not to replace types
295             (null, exps(1), exps(2), exps(3))
296               else
297               (parse(exps(0)), exps(1), exps(2), exps(3))
298         }
299       AggregateExpressions(sumType, filter, sum, count, zero, add)
300     }
301     register("agg_Expr", afx, Set(3, 4))
302 
303     register("sum_With", (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
304       val (dataType, origExp) = exps.size match {
305         case 1 => (LongType, exps(0))
306         // backwards compat
307         case 2 => (parse(exps(0)), exps(1))
308       }
309       FunN(Seq(RefExpression(dataType)), origExp, Some("sum_With"), usedAsLambda = true)
310     }, Set(1, 2))
311 
312     val ff2 = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
313       // real type for param1 is changed by aggrExpr, but last works for all compat as well
314       val (sumType, exp) =
315         exps.size match {
316           case 1 => (LongType, exps(0))
317           case 2 => (parse(exps(0)), exps(1)) // support the NO_REWRITE override case
318         }
319 
320       FunN(Seq(RefExpression(sumType), RefExpression(LongType)), exp, Some("results_With"), usedAsLambda = true)
321     }
322     register("results_With", ff2, Set(1, 2))
323 
324     val mapFX = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => exps.size match {
325       case 3 =>
326         // parse it for old sql to support backwards aggExpr
327         MapTransform.create(RefExpression(parse(exps(0))), exps(1), exps(2), zero)
328       case 2 =>
329         // default to LongType, aggrExpr must fix, 2nd param is key, third the func manipulating the key
330         MapTransform.create(RefExpression(MapType(LongType, LongType)), exps(0), exps(1), zero)
331     }
332     register("map_With", mapFX, Set(2, 3))

334     def aggFWith(fun: String) = (what: String) => (exps: Seq[Expression]) => expression(
335       if (exps.size == 0)
336         functions.expr(s"$fun( $what )")
337       else
338         functions.expr(s"$fun('${strType(exps(0))}', $what )")
339       )
340 
341     val retWith = aggFWith("results_With")
342 
343     // common cases
344     register("meanF", retWith("(sum, count) -> sum / count"), Set(0, 1))

346     val sumWith = aggFWith("sum_With")

348     val incX = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => exps match {
349       case Seq(x: AttributeReference) =>
350         val name = x.qualifier.mkString(".") + x.name // that is bad code man should be option
351         sumWith(s"sum -> sum + $name")(Seq())
352       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType)) =>
353         // case for type passing
354         sumWith("sum -> sum + 1")(exps)
355       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), x: AttributeReference) =>
356         val name = x.qualifier.mkString(".") + x.name
357         sumWith(s"sum -> sum + $name")(Seq(exps(0))) // keep the type, drop the attr
358       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), y) =>
359         qualityException(INC_REWRITE_GENEXP_ERR_MSG)
360       case Seq( y ) =>
361         val SLambdaFunction(a: Add, Seq(sum: UnresolvedNamedLambdaVariable), hidden ) = expression(functions.expr("sumWith(sum -> sum + 1)")).children(0)
362         import ShimUtils.{add => addf}
363         // could be a cast around x or three attributes plusing each other or....
364         FunN(Seq(RefExpression(LongType)),
365           SLambdaFunction(addf(a.left, y, LongType), Seq(sum), hidden )
366           , Some("inc"), usedAsLambda = true) // keep the type
367       case Seq() => expression(functions.expr(s"sumWith(sum -> sum + 1)"))
368     }
369     register("inc", incX, Set(1, 0, 2))
370 
371     // return sum
372     register("return_Sum", retWith("(sum, count) -> sum"), Set(0, 1))
373     def getRandom(exp: Expression, pos: Int) = {
374       val str = getString(exp, pos)
375       RandomSource.valueOf(str)
376     }

378     // random generators
379     val brf = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
380       //numBytes: Int, randomSource: RandomSource, seed: Long constructor but needs to use random, seed, numbytes
381       val (numBytes: Int, randomSource, seed: Long) =
382         exps.size match {
383           case 0 => (16, RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L)
384           case 1 => (16, getRandom(exps(0), 0), 0L)
385           case 2 => (16, getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
386           case 3 => (getInteger(exps(2), 2), getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
387           case _ => literalsNeeded
388         }
389 
390       RandomBytes(numBytes, randomSource, seed)
391     }
392     register("rng_Bytes", brf, Set(0,1,2,3))
393 
394     // random generators
395     val lrf = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
396 
397       //randomSource: RandomSource, seed: Long constructor but needs to use random, seed, numbytes
398       val (randomSource, seed: Long) =
399         exps.size match {
400           case 0 => (RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L)
401           case 1 => (getRandom(exps(0), 0), 0L)
402           case 2 => (getRandom(exps(0), 0), getLong(exps(1), 1))
403           case _ => literalsNeeded
404         }
405 
406       RandomLongs.create(randomSource, seed)
407     }
408     register("rng", lrf, Set(0,1,2))

427     val longPairEqual = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
428       val Seq(Literal(a, StringType), Literal(b, StringType)) = exps
429 
430       def lower(a: Any) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_lower")
431 
432       def higher(a: Any) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_higher")
433 
434       And(EqualTo(lower(a), lower(b)), EqualTo(higher(a), higher(b)))
435     }
436     register("long_Pair_Equal", longPairEqual, Set(2))
437 
438     val idEqual = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
439       val Seq(Literal(a, StringType), Literal(b, StringType)) = exps
440       def attr(a: Any, field: String) = UnresolvedAttribute(s"${a}_$field")
441 
442       And(And(EqualTo(attr(a, "base"), attr(b, "base")),
443         EqualTo(attr(a, "i0"), attr(b, "i0"))),
444         EqualTo(attr(a, "i1"), attr(b, "i1")))
445     }
446     register("id_equal", idEqual, Set(2))

452     def fieldsToHash(asStruct: Boolean, factory: String => DigestFactory) = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>
453       exps.size match {
454         case a if a > 3 =>
455           val digestImpl = getString(exps(0), 0)
456 
457           HashFunctionsExpression(exps.drop(1), digestImpl, asStruct, factory(digestImpl))
458 
459         case _ => literalsNeeded
460       }
461 
462     register("digest_To_Longs_Struct", fieldsToHash(true, MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 2)
463     register("digest_To_Longs",  fieldsToHash(false, MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 2)
464 
465     def fieldBasedID(factory: String => DigestFactory) = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>
466       exps.size match {
467         case a if a > 3 =>
468           val digestImpl = getString(exps(1), 1)
469           val prefix = getString(exps.head, 0)
470 
471           GenericLongBasedIDExpression(model.FieldBasedID,
472             HashFunctionsExpression(exps.drop(2), digestImpl, true, factory(digestImpl)), prefix)
473 
474         case _ => literalsNeeded
475       }
476 
477     register("field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(MessageDigestFactory), minimum = 3)
478     register("za_Longs_Field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(ZALongTupleHashFunctionFactory), minimum = 3)
479     register("za_Field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(ZALongHashFunctionFactory), minimum = 3)
480     register("hash_Field_Based_ID", fieldBasedID(HashFunctionFactory(_)), minimum = 3)

482     val providedID = (exps: Seq[Expression]) =>

502     val rngID = (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
503       val (randomSource, seed: Long, prefix) =
504         exps.size match {
505           case 1 => ( RandomSource.XO_RO_SHI_RO_128_PP, 0L, getString(exps.head, 0))
506           case 2 => ( getRandom(exps(1), 1), 0L,  getString(exps.head, 0))
507           case 3 => ( getRandom(exps(1), 1), getLong(exps(2), 2),  getString(exps.head, 0))
508           case _ => literalsNeeded
509         }
510 
511       GenericLongBasedIDExpression(model.RandomID,
512         RandLongsWithJump(seed, randomSource), prefix)
513     }

573     def msgAndExpr(msgDefault: String, exps: Seq[Expression]) = exps match {
574       case Seq(Literal(str: UTF8String, StringType), e: Expression) =>
575         (str.toString, e)
576       case Seq(e: Expression) =>
577         (msgDefault, e)
578     }

584     register("print_Expr", (exps: Seq[Expression]) => {
585       val (msg, exp) = msgAndExpr("Expression toStr is ->", exps)

595     register("qualityfunn", exps => {
596       val args = exps.dropRight(4)
597       val params = exps.drop(args.length)
598       val (function, name, attemptCodeGen, useAsLambda) =
599         params match {
600           case Seq(f, n, a, u) => (f, {
601             val s = getString(n)
602             if (s.isEmpty)
603               None
604             else
605               Some(s)
606           }, getBoolean(a), getBoolean(u))
607         }
608       FunN(args, function, name, attemptCodeGen = attemptCodeGen, usedAsLambda = useAsLambda)
609     }, minimum = 5)
